===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

Method code:
174 |   @Override
175 |   FlowScope flowThrough(Node n, FlowScope input) {
176 |     // If we have not walked a path from <entry> to <n>, then we don't
177 |     // want to infer anything about this scope.
178 |     if (input == bottomScope) {
179 |       return input;
180 |     }
181 | 
182 |     FlowScope output = input.createChildFlowScope();
183 |     output = traverse(n, output);
184 |     return output;
185 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,int,SourceMap,SourceMap.DetailLevel,Charset)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 591-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
591 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
592 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
593 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
594 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
595 |                                  Charset outputCharset) {
596 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
597 | 
598 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
599 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
600 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
601 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
602 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
603 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
604 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
605 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
606 |     CodeGenerator cg =
607 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
608 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
609 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
610 |     cg.add(root);
611 | 
612 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
613 | 
614 |     if (createSourceMap) {
615 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
616 |     }
617 | 
618 |     return code;
619 |   }

===== M0029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 395-410
Documentation:

Method code:
395 |     void resolveTypes() {
396 |       // Resolve types and attach them to nodes.
397 |       for (DeferredSetType deferred : deferredSetTypes) {
398 |         deferred.resolve(scope);
399 |       }
400 | 
401 |       // Resolve types and attach them to scope slots.
402 |       Iterator<Var> vars = scope.getVars();
403 |       while (vars.hasNext()) {
404 |         vars.next().resolveType(typeParsingErrorReporter);
405 |       }
406 | 
407 |       // Tell the type registry that any remaining types
408 |       // are unknown.
409 |       typeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(scope);
410 |     }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 805-829
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
805 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
806 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
807 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
808 |     }
809 | 
810 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
811 | 
812 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
813 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
814 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
815 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
816 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
817 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
818 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
819 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
820 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
821 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
822 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
823 |         } else {
824 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
825 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
826 |         }
827 |       }
828 |     }
829 |   }

Original range: 86-104
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   @Override
87 |   public boolean disables(DiagnosticGroup group) {
88 |     nextSingleton:
89 |     for (DiagnosticType type : group.getTypes()) {
90 |       DiagnosticGroup singleton = DiagnosticGroup.forType(type);
91 | 
92 |       for (WarningsGuard guard : guards) {
93 |         if (guard.disables(singleton)) {
94 |           continue nextSingleton;
95 |         } else if (guard.enables(singleton)) {
96 |           return false;
97 |         }
98 |       }
99 | 
100 |       return false;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     return true;
104 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 888-909
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
888 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
889 |     switch (n.getType()) {
890 |       case Token.CALL:
891 |       case Token.GETPROP:
892 |       case Token.GETELEM:
893 |       case Token.THROW:
894 |       case Token.NEW:
895 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
896 |       case Token.INC:
897 |       case Token.DEC:
898 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
899 |         return true;
900 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
901 |         return false;
902 |     }
903 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
904 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
905 |         return true;
906 |       }
907 |     }
908 |     return false;
909 |   }

===== M0058 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1403-1419
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1403 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1404 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1405 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1406 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1407 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1408 |         next();
1409 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1410 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1411 |           next();
1412 |         }
1413 |         skipEOLs();
1414 |         token = next();
1415 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1416 |       }
1417 |     }
1418 |     return typeExpr;
1419 |   }

===== M0071 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1726-1740
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1726 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1727 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1728 |       return getString();
1729 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1730 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1731 |       if (left == null) {
1732 |         return null;
1733 |       }
1734 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1735 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1736 |       return "this";
1737 |     } else {
1738 |       return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |   }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1865 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1866 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1867 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1868 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();

1874 |         result.first = n2clone;
1875 |       }

1877 |     }
1878 |     return result;
1879 |   }

===== M0090 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 114-154
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
114 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
115 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
116 |                 Scope functionScope,
117 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
118 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
119 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
120 |     this.compiler = compiler;
121 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
122 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
123 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
124 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
125 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
126 | 
127 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
128 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
129 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
130 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
131 |       }
132 |     }
133 | 
134 |     Iterator<Var> varIt = functionScope.getVars();
135 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
136 |       Var var = varIt.next();
137 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
138 |         continue;
139 |       }
140 | 
141 |       // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
142 |       // type is VOID.
143 |       if (var.getParentNode() != null &&
144 |           var.getType() == null && // no declared type
145 |           var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
146 |           !var.isExtern()) {
147 |         this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
148 |             var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
149 |       }
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
153 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
154 |   }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 426-478
Documentation:

Method code:
426 |     @Override
427 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
428 |       attachLiteralTypes(n);
429 | 
430 |       switch (n.getType()) {
431 |         case Token.CALL:
432 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
433 |           break;
434 | 
435 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
436 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
437 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
438 |           }
439 | 
440 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled in different branches of this
441 |           // switch statement.
442 |           if (parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
443 |               parent.getType() != Token.NAME) {
444 |             defineDeclaredFunction(n, parent);
445 |           }
446 | 
447 |           break;
448 | 
449 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
450 |           // Handle constructor and enum definitions.
451 |           defineNamedTypeAssign(n, parent);
452 | 
453 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
454 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
455 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
456 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
457 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
458 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
459 |           }
460 |           break;
461 | 
462 |         case Token.CATCH:
463 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
464 |           break;
465 | 
466 |         case Token.VAR:
467 |           defineVar(n, parent);
468 |           break;
469 | 
470 |         case Token.GETPROP:
471 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
472 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
473 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
474 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
475 |           }
476 |           break;
477 |       }
478 |     }

===== M0095 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1246-1279
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1247 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1248 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1249 |     // merge to fix this.
1250 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1251 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1252 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1253 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1254 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1255 | 
1256 |       if (value != null) {
1257 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1258 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1259 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1260 | 
1261 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1262 |         if (info == null) {
1263 |           info = varInfo;
1264 |         }
1265 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1266 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1267 |           // scope creation logic.
1268 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1269 |               t, value,
1270 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
1271 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1272 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1273 |         } else {
1274 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1275 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1276 |         }
1277 |       }
1278 |     }
1279 |   }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1361-1391
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1361 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1362 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1363 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1364 | 
1365 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1366 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1367 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1368 |       return;
1369 |     }
1370 | 
1371 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1372 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1373 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1374 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1375 | 
1376 |       // Non-native constructors should never be called directly.
1377 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1378 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType()) {
1379 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1380 |       }
1381 | 
1382 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1383 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1384 |     } else {
1385 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1386 |     }
1387 | 
1388 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1389 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1390 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1391 |   }

===== M0123 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 662-687
Documentation:

Method code:
662 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
663 |     ObjectType objectType = (ObjectType) n.getJSType();
664 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(objectType);
665 | 
666 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types.
667 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
668 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
669 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName()) {
670 |       return scope;
671 |     }
672 | 
673 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
674 |          name = name.getNext().getNext()) {
675 |       Node value = name.getNext();
676 |       scope = traverse(name, scope);
677 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
678 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getStringValue(name);
679 |       if (memberName != null) {
680 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, getJSType(value), false);
681 |       } else {
682 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
683 |         return scope;
684 |       }
685 |     }
686 |     return scope;
687 |   }

Original range: 457-461
Documentation:
/**
   * Dereference a type for property access.
   *
   * Autoboxes the type, filters null/undefined, and returns the result
   * iff it's an object.

===== M0136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1338-1373
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1338 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1339 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1340 | 
1341 |     skipEOLs();
1342 | 
1343 |     boolean restArg = false;
1344 |     token = next();
1345 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1346 |       token = next();
1347 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1348 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1349 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1350 |       }
1351 |       restArg = true;
1352 |     }
1353 | 
1354 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1355 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1356 |       skipEOLs();
1357 |       if (restArg) {
1358 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1359 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1360 |         next();
1361 |         skipEOLs();
1362 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1363 |       }
1364 | 
1365 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1366 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1367 |       } else {
1368 |         next();
1369 |       }
1370 |     }
1371 | 
1372 |     return typeNode;
1373 |   }

===== M0137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 832-854
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
832 |   private void updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
833 |     int i = 0;
834 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
835 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
836 |       JSType iParameterType = iParameter.getJSType();
837 |       if (iParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
838 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = (FunctionType) iParameterType;
839 | 
840 |         if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
841 |           // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
842 |           return;
843 |         }
844 |         Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
845 |         JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
846 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
847 |             iArgumentType instanceof FunctionType &&
848 |             iArgumentType.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
849 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
850 |         }
851 |       }
852 |       i++;
853 |     }
854 |   }

1021 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1022 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1023 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1024 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1025 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1026 |     if (var != null) {
1027 |       JSType varType = var.getType();

1029 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1030 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {

1032 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1033 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1034 |         } else {
1035 |           return varType;

1041 |     if (objType != null) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)

/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {

100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();

104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1101-1203
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1101 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1102 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1103 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1104 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1105 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1106 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1107 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1108 | 
1109 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1110 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1111 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1112 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1113 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1114 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1115 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1116 |         if (qVar != null) {
1117 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1118 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1119 |             return;
1120 |           }
1121 |           scope.undeclare(qVar);
1122 |         }
1123 |       }
1124 | 
1125 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1126 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1127 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1128 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1129 |       // 4) ASSIGN to anything else
1130 |       //
1131 |       // 1 and 3 are declarations, 4 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1132 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1133 |       //
1134 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1135 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1136 | 
1137 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3
1138 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredGetPropType(t, info, n, rhsValue);
1139 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1140 |         // Determining type for #4
1141 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1142 |       }
1143 | 
1144 |       if (valueType == null) {
1145 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1146 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1147 |               n,
1148 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1149 |               ownerName));
1150 |         }
1151 | 
1152 |         return;
1153 |       }
1154 | 
1155 |       boolean inferred = true;
1156 |       if (info != null) {
1157 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3
1158 |         inferred = !(info.hasType() || info.hasEnumParameterType() ||
1159 |             FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1160 |       }
1161 | 
1162 |       if (inferred) {
1163 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1164 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1165 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1166 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1167 |       }
1168 | 
1169 |       if (!inferred) {
1170 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1171 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1172 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1173 |           // declared yet.
1174 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1175 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1176 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1177 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1178 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1179 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1180 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern);
1181 |           }
1182 |         }
1183 | 
1184 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1185 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1186 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1187 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1188 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1189 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1190 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1191 |         if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1192 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1193 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1194 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1195 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1196 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1197 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1198 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1199 |                 true);
1200 |           }
1201 |         }
1202 |       }
1203 |     }

Method code:
404 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
405 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
406 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
407 |     switch (n.getType()) {
408 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
409 |         // normal type checking
410 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
411 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
412 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
413 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
414 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
415 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
416 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
417 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
418 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
419 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
420 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
421 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
422 |         }
423 | 
424 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
425 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
426 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
427 |         break;
428 |     }
429 |     return true;
430 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 918-929
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
918 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
919 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
920 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
921 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
922 | 
923 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
924 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
925 | 
926 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
927 |         true);
928 |     return typeNode;
929 |   }

/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
451 |     @Override
452 |     void maybeCutLine() {
453 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
454 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
455 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
456 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
457 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
458 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
459 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
460 |           lineIndex++;
461 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
462 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
463 |         } else {
464 |           startNewLine();
465 |         }
466 |       }
467 |     }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

/**
   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
191 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
192 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
193 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
194 |         objType.isInterface()) {
195 |       // Named types.

202 |               docInfo);
203 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
204 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
205 |         }
206 |       }
207 |     } else if (!objType.isNativeObjectType() &&
208 |         objType.isFunctionType()) {
209 |       // Structural functions.
210 |       objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
211 |     }
212 |   }

===== M0226 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 148-193
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
148 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
149 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
150 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
151 |       case Token.BLOCK:
152 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
153 |       case Token.TRY:
154 |       case Token.FINALLY:
155 |         return true;
156 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
157 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
158 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
159 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
160 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
161 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
162 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
163 |         // child.
164 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
165 |       case Token.WHILE:
166 |       case Token.DO:
167 |       case Token.IF:
168 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
169 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
170 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
171 | 
172 |       case Token.FOR:
173 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
174 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
175 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
176 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
177 |         // That way the following:
178 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
179 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
180 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
181 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
182 |         } else {
183 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
184 |         }
185 |       case Token.SWITCH:
186 |       case Token.CASE:
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |       case Token.WITH:
189 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
190 |       default:
191 |         return false;
192 |     }
193 |   }

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1396-1430
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1396 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1397 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1398 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1399 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1400 | 
1401 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1402 |     int ordinal = 0;
1403 |     Node parameter = null;
1404 |     Node argument = null;
1405 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1406 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1407 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1408 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1409 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1410 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1411 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1412 |       }
1413 |       argument = arguments.next();
1414 |       ordinal++;
1415 | 
1416 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1417 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1418 |     }
1419 | 
1420 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1421 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1422 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1423 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1424 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1425 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1426 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1427 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1428 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1429 |     }
1430 |   }

===== M0229 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 187-286
Documentation:

Method code:
187 |   @Override
188 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
189 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
190 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
191 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
192 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
193 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
194 |     // worthwhile.
195 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
196 |     Node condition = null;
197 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
198 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
199 | 
200 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
201 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
202 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
203 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
204 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
205 | 
206 |       switch (branch) {
207 |         case ON_TRUE:
208 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
209 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
210 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
211 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
212 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
213 |             }
214 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
215 |               FlowScope informed = output.createChildFlowScope();
216 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(),
217 |                   getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
218 |               newScope = informed;
219 |             }
220 |             break;
221 |           }
222 | 
223 |           // FALL THROUGH
224 | 
225 |         case ON_FALSE:
226 |           if (condition == null) {
227 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
228 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
229 |               condition = source;
230 | 
231 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
232 |               // of the loop.
233 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
234 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
235 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
236 |               }
237 |             }
238 |           }
239 | 
240 |           if (condition != null) {
241 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
242 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
243 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
244 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
245 |               // scope on false.
246 |               //
247 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
248 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
249 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
250 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
251 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
252 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
253 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
254 | 
255 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
256 |               // of the loop.
257 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
258 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
259 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
260 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
261 |               }
262 |               newScope =
263 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
264 |                       condition,
265 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
266 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
267 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
268 |             } else {
269 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
270 |               // of the loop.
271 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
272 |                 conditionFlowScope =
273 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
274 |               }
275 |               newScope =
276 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
277 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
278 |             }
279 |           }
280 |           break;
281 |       }
282 | 
283 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
284 |     }
285 |     return result;
286 |   }

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 670-767
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
670 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
671 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
672 |     /*
673 |      * This is the case where:
674 |      *
675 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
676 |      * the script.
677 |      *
678 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
679 |      * the caller of the function.
680 |      *
681 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
682 |      * back to the caller of the function.
683 |      *
684 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
685 |      * asked to traverse.
686 |      *
687 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
688 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
689 |      */
690 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
691 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
692 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
693 |       return null;
694 |     }
695 | 
696 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
697 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
698 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
699 |       case Token.IF:
700 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
703 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
704 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
705 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
706 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
707 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
708 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
709 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
710 |           } else {
711 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
712 |           }
713 |         } else {
714 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
715 |         }
716 |         break;
717 |       case Token.FOR:
718 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
719 |           return parent;
720 |         } else {
721 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
722 |         }
723 |       case Token.WHILE:
724 |       case Token.DO:
725 |         return parent;
726 |       case Token.TRY:
727 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
728 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
729 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
730 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
731 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
732 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
733 |           }
734 |         // CATCH block.
735 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
736 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
737 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
738 |           } else {
739 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
740 |           }
741 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
742 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
743 |           if (cfa != null) {
744 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
745 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
746 |             }
747 |           }
748 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
749 |         }
750 |     }
751 | 
752 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
753 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
754 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
755 | 
756 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
757 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
758 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
759 |     }
760 | 
761 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
762 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
763 |     } else {
764 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
765 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
766 |     }
767 |   }

===== M0235 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 682-718
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
682 |   @Override
683 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
684 |     if (this.isEquivalentTo(that)) {
685 |       return true;
686 |     }
687 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
688 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
689 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
690 |         return true;
691 |       }
692 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
693 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
694 |         return false;
695 |       }
696 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
697 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
698 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
699 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
700 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
701 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
702 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
703 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
704 |       return (this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
705 |               other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
706 |               this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis)) &&
707 |           this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
708 |     }
709 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
710 |       UnionType union = (UnionType) that;
711 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
712 |         if (this.isSubtype(element)) {
713 |           return true;
714 |         }
715 |       }
716 |     }
717 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
718 |   }

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.

   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator

Method code:
1441 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
1442 |     switch (operator) {
1443 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";

1446 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
1447 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";

1449 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
1450 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
1451 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
1452 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
1453 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
1454 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
1455 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
1456 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
1457 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
1458 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
1459 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
1460 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
1461 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
1462 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
1463 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
1464 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
1465 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
1466 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
1467 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
1468 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
1469 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
1470 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
1471 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
1472 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
1473 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
1474 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
1475 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
1476 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";
1477 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB: return "-=";
1478 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";
1479 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV: return "/=";
1480 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD: return "%=";
1481 |       case Token.VOID: return "void";
1482 |       case Token.TYPEOF: return "typeof";
1483 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF: return "instanceof";

/**
   * Given a node, determines whether that node names a prototype
   * property, and if so, returns the qualfied name node representing
   * the owner of that property. Otherwise, returns null.
   */
Method code:
297 |   private static Node getPrototypePropertyOwner(Node n) {
298 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {

===== M0259 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 614-653
Documentation:

Method code:
614 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
615 |     String varName = n.getString();
616 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
617 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
618 |     if (value != null) {
619 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
620 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
621 |           getJSType(value));
622 |       return scope;
623 |     } else {
624 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
625 |       if (var != null) {
626 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
627 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
628 | 
629 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
630 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
631 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
632 |         boolean unflowable =
633 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
634 | 
635 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
636 |         // inferred variable.
637 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
638 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
639 |             isInferred &&
640 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
641 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
642 | 
643 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
644 |           type = var.getType();
645 |           if (type == null) {
646 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
647 |           }
648 |         }
649 |       }
650 |     }
651 |     n.setJSType(type);
652 |     return scope;
653 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is inferred.
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs

   *        in live code being removed.

229 |   public final boolean defineInferredProperty(String propertyName,

/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
944 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
945 |                                       int startCharno,
946 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
947 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
948 |     Node typeNode = null;
949 | 
950 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
951 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
952 |     } else {
953 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
954 |     }
955 | 
956 |     if (typeNode != null && !matchingLC) {
957 |       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
958 |     }
959 | 
960 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
961 | 
962 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
963 |         matchingLC);
964 | 
965 |     return typeNode;
966 |   }

===== M0282 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
820 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
821 |     // unknown
822 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
823 |       return true;
824 |     }
825 |     // equality
826 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
827 |       return true;
828 |     }
829 |     // all type
830 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
831 |       return true;
832 |     }
833 |     // unions
834 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
835 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
836 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
837 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
838 |           return true;
839 |         }
840 |       }
841 |     }
842 |     // named types
843 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
844 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
845 |     }
846 |     return false;
847 |   }

/**
   * Adds a node at the left-hand side of an expression. Unlike
   * {@link #addExpr(Node,int)}, this preserves information about the context.
   *
   * The left side of an expression is special because in the JavaScript
   * grammar, certain tokens may be parsed differently when they are at
   * the beginning of a statement. For example, "{}" is parsed as a block,
   * but "{'x': 'y'}" is parsed as an object literal.

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
412 |     @Override
413 |     public final boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n,
414 |         Node parent) {
415 |       if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
416 |           n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
417 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
418 |       }
419 | 
420 |       // We do want to traverse the name of a named function, but we don't
421 |       // want to traverse the arguments or body.
422 |       return parent == null || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
423 |           n == parent.getFirstChild() || parent == scope.getRootNode();

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractBlockComment(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1223-1268
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1223 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1224 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1225 | 
1226 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1227 | 
1228 |     do {
1229 |       switch (token) {
1230 |         case ANNOTATION:
1231 |         case EOC:
1232 |         case EOF:
1233 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1234 | 
1235 |         case STAR:
1236 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1237 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1238 |               builder.append(' ');
1239 |             }
1240 | 
1241 |             builder.append('*');
1242 |           }
1243 | 
1244 |           token = next();
1245 |           continue;
1246 | 
1247 |         case EOL:
1248 |           ignoreStar = true;
1249 |           builder.append('\n');
1250 |           token = next();
1251 |           continue;
1252 | 
1253 |         default:
1254 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1255 |             builder.append(' ');
1256 |           }
1257 | 
1258 |           ignoreStar = false;
1259 | 
1260 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1261 | 
1262 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1263 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1264 |           builder.append(line);
1265 |           token = next();
1266 |       }
1267 |     } while (true);
1268 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1529-1551
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1529 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1530 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1531 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1532 |     }
1533 | 
1534 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1535 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1536 |       next();
1537 |       skipEOLs();
1538 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1539 |       if (memberType != null) {
1540 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1541 | 
1542 |         skipEOLs();
1543 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1544 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1545 |         }
1546 | 
1547 |         next();
1548 |       }
1549 |     }
1550 |     return typeName;
1551 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0318 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 202-232
Documentation:

Method code:
202 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
203 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
204 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;
205 |     if (jsDoc != null) {
206 |       nodeWithJsDoc = new NodeWithJsDoc();
207 |       nodesWithJsDoc.put(jsDoc, nodeWithJsDoc);
208 |     }
209 | 
210 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
211 |     if (nodeWithJsDoc != null) {
212 |       nodeWithJsDoc.node = irNode;
213 |     }
214 | 
215 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
216 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
217 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
218 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
219 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
220 |     } else {
221 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
222 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
223 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
224 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
225 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
226 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
227 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
228 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
229 |       }
230 |     }
231 |     return irNode;
232 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 273-316
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
273 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
274 |     if (info != null) {
275 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
276 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
277 | 
278 |       // base type
279 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
280 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
281 |           baseType = ObjectType.cast(info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
282 |           if (baseType == null) {
283 |             reportWarning(EXTENDS_NON_OBJECT, fnName, baseType.toString());
284 |           }
285 |         } else {
286 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
287 |         }
288 |       }
289 | 
290 |       // implemented interfaces
291 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
292 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
293 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
294 |           ObjectType interType = ObjectType.cast(t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
295 |           if (interType != null) {
296 |             implementedInterfaces.add(interType);
297 |           } else {
298 |             reportError(BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, fnName);
299 |           }
300 |         }
301 |         if (baseType != null) {
302 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
303 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
304 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
305 |             Iterables.addAll(
306 |                 implementedInterfaces,
307 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
308 |           }
309 |         }
310 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
311 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
312 |       }
313 |     }
314 | 
315 |     return this;
316 |   }
