===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 288-448
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
289 |     switch (n.getType()) {
290 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
291 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
292 |         break;
293 | 
294 |       case Token.NAME:
295 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
296 |         break;
297 | 
298 |       case Token.GETPROP:
299 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
300 |         break;
301 | 
302 |       case Token.AND:
303 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
304 |             .createChildFlowScope();
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.OR:
308 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
309 |             .createChildFlowScope();
310 |         break;
311 | 
312 |       case Token.HOOK:
313 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
314 |         break;
315 | 
316 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
317 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
318 |         break;
319 | 
320 |       case Token.CALL:
321 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
322 |         break;
323 | 
324 |       case Token.NEW:
325 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
326 |         break;
327 | 
328 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
329 |       case Token.ADD:
330 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
331 |         break;
332 | 
333 |       case Token.POS:
334 |       case Token.NEG:
335 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
336 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
337 |         break;
338 | 
339 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
340 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
341 |         break;
342 | 
343 |       case Token.THIS:
344 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
348 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
349 |       case Token.LSH:
350 |       case Token.RSH:
351 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
352 |       case Token.URSH:
353 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
354 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
355 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
356 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
357 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
358 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
359 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
360 |       case Token.DIV:
361 |       case Token.MOD:
362 |       case Token.BITAND:
363 |       case Token.BITXOR:
364 |       case Token.BITOR:
365 |       case Token.MUL:
366 |       case Token.SUB:
367 |       case Token.DEC:
368 |       case Token.INC:
369 |       case Token.BITNOT:
370 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
371 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
372 |         break;
373 | 
374 |       case Token.LP:
375 |       case Token.GET_REF:
376 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
377 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
378 |         break;
379 | 
380 |       case Token.COMMA:
381 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
382 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
383 |         break;
384 | 
385 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
386 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
387 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
388 |         break;
389 | 
390 |       case Token.LT:
391 |       case Token.LE:
392 |       case Token.GT:
393 |       case Token.GE:
394 |       case Token.NOT:
395 |       case Token.EQ:
396 |       case Token.NE:
397 |       case Token.SHEQ:
398 |       case Token.SHNE:
399 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
400 |       case Token.IN:
401 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
402 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
403 |         break;
404 | 
405 |       case Token.GETELEM:
406 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
407 |         break;
408 | 
409 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
410 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
411 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
412 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
413 |         }
414 |         break;
415 | 
416 |       case Token.SWITCH:
417 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case Token.VAR:
421 |       case Token.RETURN:
422 |       case Token.THROW:
423 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.CATCH:
427 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
428 |         break;
429 |     }
430 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
431 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
432 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
433 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
434 | 
435 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
436 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
437 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
438 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
439 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
440 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)

/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
390 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
391 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
392 |     if (argsParent == null) {
393 |       if (info == null) {
394 |         return this;
395 |       } else {
396 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
397 |       }
398 |     }
399 | 
400 |     // arguments
401 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
402 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
403 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
404 |     }
405 | 
406 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
407 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
408 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
409 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
410 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
411 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
412 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
413 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();

423 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&

425 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
426 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
427 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
428 |       } else {
429 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
430 |       }

434 |         if (foundTemplateType) {

436 |         }
437 |         foundTemplateType = true;
438 |       }

440 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
441 |           isOptionalParam,
442 |           isVarArgs);
443 | 
444 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
445 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
446 |       }
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
450 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
451 |     }
452 | 
453 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
454 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
455 |     }
456 | 
457 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
458 |     return this;

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>,boolean)

Original range: 1314-1463
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1314 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1315 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope, boolean forgiving) {
1316 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1317 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1318 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1319 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1320 | 
1321 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1322 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1323 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, forgiving)
1324 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1325 | 
1326 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1327 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1328 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1329 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1330 |         }
1331 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1332 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1333 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope, forgiving));
1334 | 
1335 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1336 |         return createOptionalType(
1337 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1338 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1339 | 
1340 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1341 |         return createOptionalType(
1342 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1343 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1344 | 
1345 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1346 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1347 | 
1348 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1349 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1350 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1351 | 
1352 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1353 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1354 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1355 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1356 |           builder.addAlternate(
1357 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope, false));
1358 |         }
1359 |         return builder.build();
1360 | 
1361 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1362 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1363 | 
1364 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1365 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1366 | 
1367 |       case Token.STRING:
1368 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1369 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1370 |         if (forgiving) {
1371 |           namedType.forgiveUnknownNames();
1372 |         }
1373 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1374 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1375 |         }
1376 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1377 |             !(enumTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1378 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1379 |           if (typeList != null &&
1380 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1381 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1382 |             JSType parameterType =
1383 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1384 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1385 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1386 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1387 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1388 |               JSType indexType =
1389 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1390 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1391 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1392 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1393 |             }
1394 |           }
1395 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1396 |         } else {
1397 |           return namedType;
1398 |         }
1399 | 
1400 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1401 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1402 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1403 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS) {
1404 |           Node thisNode = current.getFirstChild();
1405 |           thisType =
1406 |               ObjectType.cast(
1407 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1408 |                       thisNode, sourceName, scope, false)
1409 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1410 |           if (thisType == null) {
1411 |             reporter.warning(
1412 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject"),
1413 |                 sourceName, thisNode.getLineno(), "", thisNode.getCharno());
1414 |           }
1415 | 
1416 |           current = current.getNext();
1417 |         }
1418 | 
1419 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1420 | 
1421 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1422 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1423 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1424 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1425 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1426 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1427 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1428 |               } else {
1429 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1430 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1431 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1432 |               }
1433 |             } else {
1434 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1435 |                   arg, sourceName, scope, false);
1436 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1437 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1438 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1439 |                   reporter.warning(
1440 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1441 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1442 |                 }
1443 |               } else {
1444 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1445 |               }
1446 |             }
1447 |           }
1448 |           current = current.getNext();
1449 |         }
1450 | 
1451 |         JSType returnType =
1452 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope, false);
1453 | 
1454 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1455 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1456 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1457 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1458 |             .build();
1459 |     }
1460 | 
1461 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1462 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1463 |   }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 605-641
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
605 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
606 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
607 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
608 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
609 | 
610 |     if (existingType != null) {
611 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
612 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
613 |         FunctionType existingFn =
614 |             isInstanceObject ?
615 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
616 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
617 | 
618 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
619 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
620 |         }
621 | 
622 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
623 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
624 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
625 |         }
626 | 
627 |         return existingFn;
628 |       } else {
629 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
630 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
631 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
632 |       }
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
636 | 
637 |     if (scope.isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
638 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
639 |     }
640 |     return fnType;
641 |   }

393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }

432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 162-213
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
162 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
163 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
164 |     if (oldType == null) {
165 |       return this;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
169 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
170 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
171 |       // Not a function literal.
172 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
173 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
174 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
175 |       }
176 |     } else {
177 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
178 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
179 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
180 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
181 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
182 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
183 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
184 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
185 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
186 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
187 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
188 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
189 | 
190 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
191 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
192 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
193 | 
194 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
195 |           // arguments.
196 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
197 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
198 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
199 |           }
200 |         } else {
201 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
202 |               paramBuilder,
203 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
204 |               warnedAboutArgList,
205 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
206 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
207 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
211 |     }
212 |     return this;
213 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,JSType,boolean)

/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
872 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
873 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
874 | 
875 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualfied names.
876 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = false;
877 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
878 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
879 |             parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
880 |             parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
881 |             parent.getType() == Token.LP ||
882 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH);
883 |         shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = scope.isGlobal() &&
884 |             (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
885 |              parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
886 |       } else {
887 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
888 |             n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
889 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
890 |              parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT));
891 |       }
892 |       String variableName = n.getQualifiedName();
893 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
894 | 
895 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
896 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
897 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
898 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
899 | 
900 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
901 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
902 | 
903 |       // declared in closest scope?
904 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
905 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
906 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
907 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
908 |       } else {
909 |         if (!inferred) {
910 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
911 |         }
912 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
913 |         scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
914 | 
915 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
916 |           ObjectType globalThis =
917 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
918 |           boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
919 |           if (inferred) {
920 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
921 |                 type == null ?
922 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
923 |                     type,
924 |                 isExtern);
925 |           } else {
926 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern);
927 |           }
928 |         }
929 | 
930 |         // If we're in the global scope, also declare var.prototype
931 |         // in the scope chain.
932 |         if (scopeToDeclareIn.isGlobal() && type instanceof FunctionType) {
933 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
934 |           if (fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) {
935 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
936 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
937 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), compiler.getInput(sourceName),
938 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
939 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
940 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
941 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
942 |           }
943 |         }
944 |       }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)

Original range: 117-138
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
117 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
118 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
119 |     String methodName = null;
120 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
121 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
122 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
123 |       String name = callName.getString();
124 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
125 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
126 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
127 |       }
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     if (methodName != null) {
131 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
132 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
133 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
134 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 |     return null;
138 |   }

/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
210 |   public int getMinArguments() {
211 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
212 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
213 |     // of the last required parameter.
214 |     int i = 0;
215 |     int min = 0;
216 |     for (Node n : getParameters()) {
217 |       i++;
218 |       if (!n.isOptionalArg() && !n.isVarArgs()) {
219 |         min = i;
220 |       }
221 |     }
222 |     return min;
223 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 774-788
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
774 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
775 |     switch (n.getType()) {
776 |       case Token.DO:
777 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
778 |       case Token.FOR:
779 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
780 |           return n;
781 |         }
782 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
783 |       case Token.LABEL:
784 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
785 |       default:
786 |         return n;
787 |     }
788 |   }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 491-536
Documentation:

Method code:
491 |     JSType getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String sourceName,
492 |         Node node, JSDocInfo info) {
493 |       JSType jsType = null;
494 |       Node objNode = node.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
495 |           node.getFirstChild() : null;
496 |       if (info != null) {
497 |         if (info.hasType()) {
498 |           jsType = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
499 |         } else if (FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
500 |           String fnName = node.getQualifiedName();
501 | 
502 |           // constructors are often handled separately.
503 |           if (info.isConstructor() && typeRegistry.getType(fnName) != null) {
504 |             return null;
505 |           }
506 | 
507 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
508 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(
509 |                   fnName, compiler, node, sourceName, scope)
510 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
511 |               .inferReturnType(info)
512 |               .inferParameterTypes(info)
513 |               .inferInheritance(info);
514 | 
515 |           // Infer the context type.
516 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
517 |           if (objNode != null) {
518 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
519 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
520 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
521 |               searchedForThisType = true;
522 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
523 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
524 |               searchedForThisType = true;
525 |             }
526 |           }
527 | 
528 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
529 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
530 |           }
531 | 
532 |           jsType = builder.buildAndRegister();
533 |         }
534 |       }
535 |       return jsType;
536 |     }

===== M0425 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 69-110
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
71 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
72 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
73 |     if (type != null) {
74 |       Node subclass = null;
75 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
76 | 
77 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
78 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
79 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
80 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
81 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
83 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       if (callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
85 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
86 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
87 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
88 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
89 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
90 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
94 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
95 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
96 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
97 |       if (subclass != null &&
98 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
99 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
100 |         // make sure to strip the prototype off of the nodes
101 |         // to normalize for goog.mixin
102 |         return new SubclassRelationship(
103 |             type,
104 |             stripPrototype(subclass),
105 |             stripPrototype(superclass));
106 |       }
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     return null;
110 |   }

===== M0431 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 138-184
Documentation:

Method code:
138 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
139 |                                    String sourceString,
140 |                                    Config config,
141 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
142 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
143 |         config, errorReporter);
144 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
145 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
146 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
147 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
148 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
149 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
150 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
151 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
152 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
153 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
154 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
155 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
156 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
157 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
158 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
159 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
160 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
161 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
162 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
163 |           } else {
164 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
165 |             if (info != null) {
166 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
167 |             }
168 |           }
169 |         }
170 |       }
171 | 
172 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
173 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
174 |       // to that node.
175 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
176 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
177 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
178 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
179 |         }
180 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
181 |       }
182 |     }
183 |     return irNode;
184 |   }

   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.

Method code:
399 |   @Override
400 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
401 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
402 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
403 |     if (unknown) {
404 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
405 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
406 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
407 |         unknown = false;
408 |       } else {
409 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
410 |       }
411 |     }
412 |     return unknown;
413 |   }

===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1724-1734
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1724 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1725 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1726 |     int len = s.length();
1727 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1728 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1729 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1730 |         return false;
1731 |       }
1732 |     }
1733 |     return true;
1734 |   }

===== M0444 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 442-788
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
442 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     JSType childType;
444 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
445 |     Node left, right;
446 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
447 |     boolean typeable = true;
448 | 
449 |     switch (n.getType()) {
450 |       case Token.NAME:
451 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.LP:
455 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
456 |         // ignored here.
457 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
458 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
459 |         } else {
460 |           typeable = false;
461 |         }
462 |         break;
463 | 
464 |       case Token.COMMA:
465 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
466 |         break;
467 | 
468 |       case Token.TRUE:
469 |       case Token.FALSE:
470 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.THIS:
474 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
475 |         break;
476 | 
477 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
478 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
479 |         break;
480 | 
481 |       case Token.GET_REF:
482 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
483 |         break;
484 | 
485 |       case Token.NULL:
486 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
487 |         break;
488 | 
489 |       case Token.NUMBER:
490 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
491 |           ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
492 |         } else {
493 |           typeable = false;
494 |         }
495 |         break;
496 | 
497 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
498 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.STRING:
502 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
503 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
504 |         } else {
505 |           typeable = false;
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 | 
509 |       case Token.REGEXP:
510 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
511 |         break;
512 | 
513 |       case Token.GETPROP:
514 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
515 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
516 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
517 |         break;
518 | 
519 |       case Token.GETELEM:
520 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
521 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
522 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
523 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
524 |         typeable = false;
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.VAR:
528 |         visitVar(t, n);
529 |         typeable = false;
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.NEW:
533 |         visitNew(t, n);
534 |         typeable = true;
535 |         break;
536 | 
537 |       case Token.CALL:
538 |         visitCall(t, n);
539 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.RETURN:
543 |         visitReturn(t, n);
544 |         typeable = false;
545 |         break;
546 | 
547 |       case Token.DEC:
548 |       case Token.INC:
549 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
550 |         validator.expectNumber(
551 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
552 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
553 |         break;
554 | 
555 |       case Token.NOT:
556 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
557 |         break;
558 | 
559 |       case Token.VOID:
560 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
564 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.BITNOT:
568 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
569 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
570 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
571 |               childType.toString());
572 |         }
573 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
574 |         break;
575 | 
576 |       case Token.POS:
577 |       case Token.NEG:
578 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
579 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
580 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
581 |         break;
582 | 
583 |       case Token.EQ:
584 |       case Token.NE: {
585 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
586 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
587 | 
588 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
589 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
590 |         TernaryValue result =
591 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
592 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
593 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
594 |             result = result.not();
595 |           }
596 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
597 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
598 |         }
599 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
600 |         break;
601 |       }
602 | 
603 |       case Token.SHEQ:
604 |       case Token.SHNE: {
605 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
606 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
607 | 
608 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
609 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
610 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
611 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
612 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
613 |               rightType.toString());
614 |         }
615 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
616 |         break;
617 |       }
618 | 
619 |       case Token.LT:
620 |       case Token.LE:
621 |       case Token.GT:
622 |       case Token.GE:
623 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
624 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
625 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
626 |           validator.expectNumber(
627 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
628 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
629 |           validator.expectNumber(
630 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
631 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
632 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
633 |           // OK.
634 |         } else {
635 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
636 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
637 |           // should match a string context.
638 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
639 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
640 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
641 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
642 |           message = "right side of comparison";
643 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
644 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
645 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
646 |         }
647 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
648 |         break;
649 | 
650 |       case Token.IN:
651 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
652 |         right = n.getLastChild();
653 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
654 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
655 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
656 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
657 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
658 |         break;
659 | 
660 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
661 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
662 |         right = n.getLastChild();
663 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
664 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
665 | 
666 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
667 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
668 |         validator.expectActualObject(
669 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
670 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
671 |         break;
672 | 
673 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
674 |         visitAssign(t, n);
675 |         typeable = false;
676 |         break;
677 | 
678 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
679 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
680 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
681 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
682 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
683 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
684 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
685 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
686 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
688 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
689 |       case Token.LSH:
690 |       case Token.RSH:
691 |       case Token.URSH:
692 |       case Token.DIV:
693 |       case Token.MOD:
694 |       case Token.BITOR:
695 |       case Token.BITXOR:
696 |       case Token.BITAND:
697 |       case Token.SUB:
698 |       case Token.ADD:
699 |       case Token.MUL:
700 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.DELPROP:
704 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
705 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
706 |         }
707 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.CASE:
711 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
712 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
713 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
714 |         typeable = false;
715 |         break;
716 | 
717 |       case Token.WITH: {
718 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
719 |         childType = getJSType(child);
720 |         validator.expectObject(
721 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
722 |         typeable = false;
723 |         break;
724 |       }
725 | 
726 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
727 |         visitFunction(t, n);
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
731 |       case Token.LABEL:
732 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
733 |       case Token.SWITCH:
734 |       case Token.BREAK:
735 |       case Token.CATCH:
736 |       case Token.TRY:
737 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
738 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
739 |       case Token.BLOCK:
740 |       case Token.EMPTY:
741 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
742 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
743 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
744 |       case Token.THROW:
745 |         typeable = false;
746 |         break;
747 | 
748 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
749 |       case Token.DO:
750 |       case Token.FOR:
751 |       case Token.IF:
752 |       case Token.WHILE:
753 |         typeable = false;
754 |         break;
755 | 
756 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
757 |       case Token.AND:
758 |       case Token.HOOK:
759 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
760 |       case Token.OR:
761 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
762 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
763 |         } else {
764 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
765 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
766 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
767 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
768 |           } else {
769 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
770 |           }
771 |         }
772 |         break;
773 | 
774 |       default:
775 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
776 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
777 |         break;
778 |     }
779 | 
780 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
781 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
782 | 
783 |     if (typeable) {
784 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
785 |     }
786 | 
787 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
788 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
297 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
298 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
299 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
300 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
301 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
302 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
303 |         // the line numbers of both types.
304 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType);
305 |       } else {
306 |         mismatch(t, n,
307 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
308 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
309 |             rightType, leftType);
310 |       }
311 |       return false;
312 |     }
313 |     return true;
314 |   }

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.FlatFlowScopeCache.FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 419-483
Documentation:
// A cache at the join of two scope chains.
Method code:
419 |     FlatFlowScopeCache(LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeA,
420 |         LinkedFlowScope joinedScopeB) {
421 |       linkedEquivalent = null;
422 | 
423 |       // Always prefer the "real" function scope to the faked-out
424 |       // bottom scope.
425 |       functionScope = joinedScopeA.flowsFromBottom() ?
426 |           joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope() : joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope();
427 | 
428 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsA = joinedScopeA.allFlowSlots();
429 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slotsB = joinedScopeB.allFlowSlots();
430 | 
431 |       symbols = slotsA;
432 | 
433 |       // There are 5 different join cases:
434 |       // 1) The type is declared in joinedScopeA, not in joinedScopeB,
435 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in A.
436 |       // 2) The type is declared in joinedScopeB, not in joinedScopeA,
437 |       //    and not in functionScope. Just use the one in B.
438 |       // 3) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeA, but
439 |       //    not in joinedScopeB. Join the two types.
440 |       // 4) The type is declared in functionScope and joinedScopeB, but
441 |       //    not in joinedScopeA. Join the two types.
442 |       // 5) The type is declared in joinedScopeA and joinedScopeB. Join
443 |       //    the two types.
444 |       Set<String> symbolNames = Sets.newHashSet(symbols.keySet());
445 |       symbolNames.addAll(slotsB.keySet());
446 | 
447 |       for (String name : symbolNames) {
448 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotA = slotsA.get(name);
449 |         StaticSlot<JSType> slotB = slotsB.get(name);
450 | 
451 |         JSType joinedType = null;
452 |         if (slotB == null || slotB.getType() == null) {
453 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
454 |               = joinedScopeB.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
455 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
456 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
457 |             // Case #1 -- already inserted.
458 |           } else {
459 |             // Case #3
460 |             joinedType = slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
461 |           }
462 |         } else if (slotA == null || slotA.getType() == null) {
463 |           StaticSlot<JSType> fnSlot
464 |               = joinedScopeA.getFunctionScope().getSlot(name);
465 |           JSType fnSlotType = fnSlot == null ? null : fnSlot.getType();
466 |           if (fnSlotType == null) {
467 |             // Case #2
468 |             symbols.put(name, slotB);
469 |           } else {
470 |             // Case #4
471 |             joinedType = slotB.getType().getLeastSupertype(fnSlotType);
472 |           }
473 |         } else {
474 |           // Case #5
475 |           joinedType =
476 |               slotA.getType().getLeastSupertype(slotB.getType());
477 |         }
478 | 
479 |         if (joinedType != null) {
480 |           symbols.put(name, new SimpleSlot(name, joinedType, true));
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |     }

1484 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {

===== M0470 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 382-397
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
382 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
383 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
384 |       Set<String> directives = null;
385 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
386 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
387 |         if (directives == null) {
388 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
389 |         } else {
390 |           directives.add(directive);
391 |         }
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       if (directives != null) {
395 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
396 |       }
397 |     }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 948-968
Documentation:

Method code:
948 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
949 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
950 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
951 |       return s;
952 |     }
953 | 
954 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
955 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
956 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
957 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
958 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
959 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
960 |       // crazy.
961 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
962 |         sb.append(c);
963 |       } else {
964 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
965 |       }
966 |     }
967 |     return sb.toString();
968 |   }

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 212-287
Documentation:

Method code:
212 |   @Override
213 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
214 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
215 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
216 | 
217 |     switch (n.getType()) {
218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
219 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
220 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
221 |           return true;
222 |         }
223 |         return false;
224 |       case Token.TRY:
225 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |         return true;
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     /*
230 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
231 |      * is.
232 |      *
233 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
234 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
235 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
236 |      *
237 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
238 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
239 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
240 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
241 |      *
242 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
243 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
244 |      */
245 |     if (parent != null) {
246 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
247 |         case Token.FOR:
248 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
249 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
250 | 
251 |         // Skip the conditions.
252 |         case Token.IF:
253 |         case Token.WHILE:
254 |         case Token.WITH:
255 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
256 |         case Token.DO:
257 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
258 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
259 |         case Token.SWITCH:
260 |         case Token.CASE:
261 |         case Token.CATCH:
262 |         case Token.LABEL:
263 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
264 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
265 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
266 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
267 |         case Token.BREAK:
268 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
269 |         case Token.VAR:
270 |         case Token.RETURN:
271 |         case Token.THROW:
272 |           return false;
273 |         case Token.TRY:
274 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
275 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
276 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
277 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
278 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
279 |            */
280 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
281 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
282 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
283 |           }
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 |     return true;
287 |   }

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .

739 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
740 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
741 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
742 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
743 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
744 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
745 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
746 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;

748 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;

===== M0493 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 530-572
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
530 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
531 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
532 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
533 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
534 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
535 |     if (objectType == null) {
536 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
537 |     } else {
538 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
539 |         return;
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
543 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
544 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
545 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
546 |         //
547 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
548 |         //    inferred type.
549 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
550 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
551 |         //    define it.
552 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
553 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
554 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
555 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
556 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
557 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
558 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
559 |           } else {
560 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
561 |           }
562 |         } else {
563 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
564 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
565 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
566 |           } else {
567 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
568 |           }
569 |         }
570 |       }
571 |     }
572 |   }

===== M0498 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 477-516
Documentation:

Method code:
477 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
478 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
479 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
480 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
481 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
482 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
483 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
484 |       return;
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
488 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
489 | 
490 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
491 |     // function (unless it is called)
492 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
493 |       child = child.getNext();
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     if (child != null) {
497 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
498 |     } else {
499 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
500 |     }
501 | 
502 |     // Synthetic blocks
503 |     if (parent != null) {
504 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
505 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
506 |         case Token.CASE:
507 |         case Token.TRY:
508 |           break;
509 |         default:
510 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
511 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
512 |           }
513 |           break;
514 |       }
515 |     }
516 |   }

===== M0499 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1497-1522
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1497 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1498 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1499 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1500 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1501 |       skipEOLs();
1502 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1503 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1504 |       skipEOLs();
1505 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1506 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1507 |       skipEOLs();
1508 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1509 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1510 |       String string = stream.getString();
1511 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1512 |         skipEOLs();
1513 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1514 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1515 |         return newStringNode(string);
1516 |       } else {

1518 |       }
1519 |     }
1520 | 
1521 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1522 |   }

===== M0500 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1224-1254
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1224 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1225 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1226 |         Node n = stub.node;
1227 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1228 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1229 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1230 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1231 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1232 | 
1233 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1234 |           continue;
1235 |         }
1236 | 
1237 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1238 |         // in the type registry.
1239 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1240 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1241 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1242 | 
1243 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1244 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1245 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1246 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1247 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1248 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern);
1249 |         } else {
1250 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1251 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1252 |         }
1253 |       }
1254 |     }

===== M0512 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 236-267
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
236 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
237 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
238 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
239 |       return this;
240 |     }
241 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
242 |     if (returnType == null) {
243 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
244 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
245 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
246 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
247 |         int cType = current.getType();
248 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null ||
249 |             cType == Token.THROW) {
250 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
251 |           break;
252 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
253 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
254 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
255 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
256 |             worklist.add(child);
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
262 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
263 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
264 |       }
265 |     }
266 |     return this;
267 |   }

444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;

===== M0535 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }
