/**
     * Defines a qualified name assign to an enum or constructor.
     */
Method code:
601 |     void defineNamedTypeAssign(Node n, Node parent) {
602 |       assertDefinitionNode(n, Token.ASSIGN);
603 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
604 | 
605 |       // TODO(nicksantos): We should support direct assignment to a
606 |       // prototype, as in:
607 |       // Foo.prototype = {
608 |       //   a: function() { ... },
609 |       //   b: function() { ... }
610 |       // };
611 |       // Right now (6/23/08), we understand most of this syntax, but we
612 |       // don't tie the "a" and "b" methods to the context of Foo.
613 | 
614 |       Node rvalue = n.getLastChild();
615 |       Node lvalue = n.getFirstChild();
616 |       info = (info != null) ? info : rvalue.getJSDocInfo();
617 |       if (rvalue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
618 |           info != null && info.isConstructor()) {
619 |         getFunctionType(lvalue.getQualifiedName(), rvalue, info,
620 |             lvalue);
621 |       } else if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
622 |         JSType type = getEnumType(lvalue.getQualifiedName(), n, rvalue,
623 |             info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
624 |         if (type != null) {
625 |           setDeferredType(lvalue, type);
626 |         }
627 |       }
628 |     }

===== M0540 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

===== M0541 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0542 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2127-2142
Documentation:
/**
   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.
   */
Method code:
2127 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2128 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2129 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2130 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2131 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.
2132 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
2133 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
2134 |         // on ASSIGNs
2135 |         fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
2136 |       } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
2137 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };
2138 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2139 |       }
2140 |     }
2141 |     return fnInfo;
2142 |   }

   * @param item The item to inspect

Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {
777 |       return null;
778 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
779 |       return item.next;

781 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
782 |       if (result != item.next) {
783 |         return new PropListItem(
784 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);

786 |         return item;
787 |       }
788 |     }
789 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1136-1162
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1136 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1137 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1138 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1139 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1140 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1141 |       return;
1142 |     }
1143 | 
1144 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1145 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1146 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1147 |     // and what isn't.
1148 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1149 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1150 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1151 | 
1152 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1153 |     // non-object.
1154 |     if (!validator.expectNotVoid(t, n, childType,
1155 |             "undefined has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1156 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1157 |       return;
1158 |     }
1159 | 
1160 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1161 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1162 |   }

===== M0583 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

/**
   * Make sure that the access of this property is ok.

1167 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1168 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1169 |     ObjectType objectType = childType.dereference();

===== M0597 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 584-610
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
584 |   @Override
585 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
586 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
587 |       return false;
588 |     }
589 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
590 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
591 |       return false;
592 |     }
593 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
594 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
595 |         return this == that;
596 |       }
597 |       return false;
598 |     }
599 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
600 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
601 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
602 |       }
603 |       return false;
604 |     }
605 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
606 |       return false;
607 |     }
608 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
609 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
610 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 191-210
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
191 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
192 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
193 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
194 |     worklist.add(entry);
195 | 
196 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
197 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
198 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
199 |         continue;
200 |       }
201 | 
202 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
203 | 
204 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
205 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
206 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
207 |         worklist.add(candidate);
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 |   }

Method code:
425 |     @Override
426 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
427 |       if (lineBreak) {
428 |         if (sawFunction) {
429 |           startNewLine();
430 |           sawFunction = false;
431 |         }
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
435 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
436 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
437 |       int len = code.length();
438 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
439 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
440 |         if (ch == ';') {
441 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
442 |         }
443 |       }
444 |       maybeCutLine();
445 |     }

123 |   public int getPropertiesCount() {

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.

Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-613
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
588 |     ObjectType owner = null;
589 | 
590 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
591 |     // to discrete object types.
592 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
593 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
594 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
595 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
596 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
597 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
598 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (owner == null) {
603 |       return;
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
607 |     if (typeSet == null) {
608 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
609 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
610 |     }
611 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
612 |     typeSet.add(owner);
613 |   }

===== M0627 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.allFlowSlots()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 347-363
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets all the symbols that have been defined before this point
   * in the current flow. Does not return slots that have not changed during
   * the flow.
   *
   * For example, consider the code:
   * <code>
   * var x = 3;
   * function f() {
   *   var y = 5;
   *   y = 6; // FLOW POINT
   *   var z = y;
   *   return z;
   * }
   * </code>
   * A FlowScope at FLOW POINT will return a slot for y, but not
   * a slot for x or z.
   */
Method code:
347 |   private Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> allFlowSlots() {
348 |     Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> slots = Maps.newHashMap();
349 |     for (LinkedFlowSlot slot = lastSlot;
350 |          slot != null; slot = slot.parent) {
351 |       if (!slots.containsKey(slot.getName())) {
352 |         slots.put(slot.getName(), slot);
353 |       }
354 |     }
355 | 
356 |     for (String key : cache.symbols.keySet()) {
357 |       if (!slots.containsKey(key)) {
358 |         slots.put(key, cache.symbols.get(key));
359 |       }
360 |     }
361 | 
362 |     return slots;
363 |   }

===== M0630 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

===== M0641 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 511-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
512 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
513 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
514 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
515 |     if (isOptional) {
516 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
517 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
518 |       // error can be reported.
519 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
520 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
521 |         emittedWarning = true;
522 |       }
523 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
524 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
525 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
526 |         emittedWarning = true;
527 |       }
528 |     } else {
529 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
530 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
531 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
532 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
533 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
534 |         } else {
535 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
536 |         }
537 |         emittedWarning = true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return emittedWarning;
541 |   }

/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
315 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
316 |       Node ret = transform(n);
317 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
318 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
319 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
320 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
321 |       }
322 |       return ret;
323 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1095 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1096 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1097 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1098 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1099 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1100 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1101 |       } else {
1102 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1103 |       }
1104 |     }
1105 |     return builder.build();
1106 |   }

===== M0653 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 337-371
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
337 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
338 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
339 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
340 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
341 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
342 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
343 |     }
344 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
345 |       // TODO(user): Doing an instanceof check here is too
346 |       // restrictive as (Date,Error) is, for instance, an object type
347 |       // even though its implementation is a UnionType. Would need to
348 |       // create interfaces JSType, ObjectType, FunctionType etc and have
349 |       // separate implementation instead of the class hierarchy, so that
350 |       // union types can also be object types, etc.
351 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
352 |     } else if (owner != null &&
353 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
354 |       // If the function is of the form:
355 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
356 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
357 |       // if it's of the form:
358 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
359 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
360 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
361 |       ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(
362 |           typeRegistry.getType(
363 |               scope, ownerTypeName, sourceName,
364 |               owner.getLineno(), owner.getCharno()));
365 |       if (ownerType != null) {
366 |         thisType = ownerType;
367 |       }
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     return this;
371 |   }

   */
Method code:
1112 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
1113 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1114 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1115 | 
1116 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1117 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1118 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1119 |     }
1120 | 
1121 |     stream.update();
1122 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1123 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1124 | 
1125 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1126 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1127 | 
1128 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1129 |       line = line.trim();
1130 |     }
1131 | 
1132 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1133 |     builder.append(line);
1134 | 
1135 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1136 |     token = next();
1137 | 
1138 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1139 | 
1140 |     do {
1141 |       switch (token) {
1142 |         case STAR:
1143 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1144 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1145 |               builder.append(' ');
1146 |             }
1147 | 
1148 |             builder.append('*');
1149 |           }
1150 | 
1151 |           token = next();
1152 |           continue;
1153 | 
1154 |         case EOL:
1155 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1156 |             builder.append("\n");
1157 |           }
1158 | 
1159 |           ignoreStar = true;
1160 |           token = next();
1161 |           continue;
1162 | 
1163 |         case ANNOTATION:
1164 |         case EOC:
1165 |         case EOF:
1166 |           // When we're capturing a license block, annotations
1167 |           // in the block are ok.
1168 |           if (!(option == WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE &&
1169 |                 token == JsDocToken.ANNOTATION)) {
1170 |             String multilineText = builder.toString();
1171 | 
1172 |             if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1173 |               multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1174 |             }
1175 | 
1176 |             int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1177 |             int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1178 | 
1179 |             if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1180 |               jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1181 |                   endLineno, endCharno);
1182 |             }
1183 | 
1184 |             return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1185 |           }
1186 | 
1187 |           // FALL THROUGH
1188 | 
1189 |         default:
1190 |           ignoreStar = false;
1191 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1192 | 
1193 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1194 |             builder.append(' ');
1195 |           }
1196 | 
1197 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1198 | 
1199 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1200 | 
1201 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1202 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1203 |           }
1204 | 
1205 |           builder.append(line);
1206 |           token = next();
1207 |       }
1208 |     } while (true);
1209 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
1418 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
1419 |     if (node.getType() == Token.STRING && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1420 |       int index = 0;
1421 |       for (Node current = parent.getFirstChild();
1422 |            current != null;
1423 |            current = current.getNext()) {
1424 |         if (current == node) {
1425 |           return index % 2 == 0;
1426 |         }
1427 |         index++;
1428 |       }
1429 |     }
1430 |     return false;
1431 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
782 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
783 |       Node callNode) {
784 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
785 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
786 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
787 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
788 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
789 |       return scope;
790 |     }
791 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
792 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
793 |       return scope;
794 |     }
795 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
796 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
797 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
798 |     if (assertedType == null) {
799 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
800 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
801 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
802 |         if (type != narrowed) {
803 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
804 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
805 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
806 |         }
807 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
808 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
809 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
810 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
811 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
812 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
813 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
814 |       }
815 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
816 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
817 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
818 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
819 |       if (type != narrowed) {
820 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
821 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
822 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
823 |       }
824 |     }
825 |     return scope;
826 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1022-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1022 |   Node parseInputs() {
1023 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1024 | 
1025 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1026 |     // individual file parse trees.
1027 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1028 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1029 |     }
1030 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1031 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1032 |     }
1033 | 
1034 |     // Parse main js sources.
1035 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1036 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1037 | 
1038 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1039 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1040 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1041 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1042 |     }
1043 | 
1044 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1045 | 
1046 |     try {
1047 |       // Parse externs sources.
1048 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1049 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1050 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1051 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1052 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1053 |           return null;
1054 |         }
1055 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1056 |       }
1057 | 
1058 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1059 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1060 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1061 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1062 | 
1063 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1064 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1065 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1066 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1067 |           }
1068 |         }
1069 | 
1070 |         try {
1071 |           inputs =
1072 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1073 |               .manageDependencies(inputs);
1074 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1075 |           report(JSError.make(
1076 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1077 |           return null;
1078 |         }
1079 |       }
1080 | 
1081 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1082 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1083 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1084 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1085 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1086 |           return null;
1087 |         }
1088 | 
1089 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1090 |         if (n == null) {
1091 |           continue;
1092 |         }
1093 | 
1094 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1095 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1096 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1097 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1098 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1099 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1100 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1101 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1102 | 
1103 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1104 | 
1105 |             externs.add(input);
1106 |             staleInputs = true;
1107 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1108 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1109 |             staleInputs = true;
1110 |           }
1111 |         }
1112 |       }
1113 | 
1114 |       if (staleInputs) {
1115 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1116 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1117 |       }
1118 | 
1119 |       // Build the AST.
1120 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1121 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1122 |         if (n == null) {
1123 |           continue;
1124 |         }
1125 | 
1126 |         if (devMode) {
1127 |           runSanityCheck();
1128 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1129 |             return null;
1130 |           }
1131 |         }
1132 | 
1133 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1134 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1135 | 
1136 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1137 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1138 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1139 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1140 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1141 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1145 |       }
1146 | 
1147 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1148 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1149 | 
1150 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1151 |     } finally {
1152 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 861-944
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
861 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
862 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
863 | 
864 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
865 |       return;
866 |     }
867 | 
868 |     int i = 0;
869 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
870 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
871 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
872 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
873 |       iParameterType = iParameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
874 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
875 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
876 |         JSType iArgumentType = null;
877 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeArgument = false;
878 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
879 |           foundTemplateTypeArgument = true;
880 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
881 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
882 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
883 |             compiler.report(
884 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
885 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
886 |             return;
887 |           }
888 |         }
889 | 
890 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
891 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
892 |         int j = 0;
893 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
894 |           JSType jParameterType = getJSType(jParameter);
895 |           if (jParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
896 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = (FunctionType) jParameterType;
897 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
898 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
899 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
900 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
901 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
902 |                 return;
903 |               }
904 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
905 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
906 |               if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
907 |                   jArgumentType instanceof FunctionType) {
908 |                 if (foundTemplateTypeArgument) {
909 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
910 |                   // using the actual type of T.
911 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = (FunctionType) jArgumentType;
912 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
913 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
914 |                     // function.
915 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
916 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
917 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
918 |                   }
919 |                 } else {
920 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
921 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
922 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
923 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
924 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
925 |                   }
926 |                 }
927 |               }
928 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
929 |               // types of the arguments match.
930 |             }
931 |           }
932 |           j++;
933 |         }
934 | 
935 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
936 |           Node source = fnType.getSource();
937 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(source), source,
938 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
939 |           return;
940 |         }
941 |       }
942 |       i++;
943 |     }
944 |   }

/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
183 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
184 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
185 | 
186 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
187 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.

194 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
195 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&

198 |       }

===== M0724 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 144-185
Documentation:

Method code:
144 |   @Override
145 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
146 |     this.root = root;
147 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
148 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
149 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
150 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities);
151 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
152 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
153 | 
154 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
155 |     // search on the CFG.
156 |     priorityCounter = 0;
157 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
158 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
159 | 
160 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
161 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
162 |       // priority of them too.
163 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
164 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
165 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
166 |           Preconditions.checkState(
167 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
168 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
169 |         }
170 |       }
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
174 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
175 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
176 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
177 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
178 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
179 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
180 |       }
181 |     }
182 | 
183 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
184 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
185 |   }

/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
463 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
464 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
465 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
466 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
467 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
468 |       if (info == null) {
469 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
470 |       }
471 |       allowDupe =
472 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
476 | 
477 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
478 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
479 |     // compilation process.
480 |     if (varType != null &&
481 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
482 |         newType != null &&
483 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
484 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
485 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
486 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
487 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
488 |       // native type.
489 |       if (var.input == null) {
490 |         n.setJSType(varType);
491 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
492 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
493 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
494 |           }
495 |         } else {
496 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
497 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
498 |         }
499 |       } else {
500 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
501 |         // match the original type.
502 |         //
503 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
504 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
505 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
506 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
507 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
508 |           if (shouldReport) {
509 |             compiler.report(
510 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
511 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
512 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
513 |                     varType.toString()));
514 |           }
515 |         }
516 |       }
517 |     }
518 |   }

===== M0729 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1678-1702
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1678 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1679 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1680 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1681 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1682 |             type.isUnknownType());
1683 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1684 |     if (info != null) {
1685 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1686 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1687 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1688 |         type = infoType;
1689 |       }
1690 | 
1691 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1692 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1693 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1694 |         compiler.report(
1695 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1696 |       }
1697 |     }
1698 | 
1699 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1700 |       n.setJSType(type);
1701 |     }
1702 |   }

===== M0730 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

/**
     * Defines a variable based on the {@link Token#NAME} node passed.
     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.
     * @param var The parent of the {@code name} node, which must be a
     *     {@link Token#VAR} node.
     * @param parent {@code var}'s parent.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} information relating to this
     *     {@code name} node.
     */
Method code:
639 |     private void defineName(Node name, Node var, Node parent, JSDocInfo info) {
640 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
641 | 
642 |       if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
643 |         // function
644 |         String functionName = name.getString();
645 |         FunctionType functionType =
646 |             getFunctionType(functionName, value, info, null);
647 |         if (functionType.isReturnTypeInferred() &&
648 |             scope.isLocal()) {
649 |           defineSlot(name, var, null);
650 |         } else {
651 |           defineSlot(name, var, functionType);
652 |         }
653 |       } else {
654 |         // variable's type
655 |         JSType type = null;
656 |         if (info == null) {
657 |           // the variable's type will be inferred
658 |           CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
659 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(input, sourceName);
660 |           type = input.isExtern() ?
661 |               getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : null;
662 |         } else if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
663 |           type = getEnumType(name.getString(), var, value,
664 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
665 |         } else if (info.isConstructor()) {
666 |           type = getFunctionType(name.getString(), value, info, name);
667 |         } else {
668 |           type = getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, name, info);
669 |         }
670 | 
671 |         defineSlot(name, var, type);
672 |       }
673 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 156-199
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
156 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
157 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
158 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
159 |     // show up in the type registry.
160 |     Scope newScope = null;
161 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
162 |     if (parent == null) {
163 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
164 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
165 | 
166 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
167 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
168 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
169 |     } else {
170 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
171 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
172 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
173 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
177 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
178 | 
179 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
180 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
181 |     // build properties on.
182 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
183 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
184 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
185 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
186 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
187 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
188 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
189 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 | 
194 |     if (parent == null) {
195 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
196 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
197 |     }
198 |     return newScope;
199 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

===== M0777 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1315-1352
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1315 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1316 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1317 | 
1318 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1319 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1320 |     if (functionType.isInterface() || functionType.isConstructor()) {
1321 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1322 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1323 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1324 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1325 |           (baseConstructor.isConstructor() && functionType.isInterface() ||
1326 |            baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1327 |         compiler.report(
1328 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1329 |       }
1330 | 
1331 |       for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1332 |         boolean badImplementedType = false;
1333 |         ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1334 |         if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1335 |           FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1336 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1337 |           if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1338 |               !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1339 |             badImplementedType = true;
1340 |           }
1341 |         } else {
1342 |           badImplementedType = true;
1343 |         }
1344 |         if (badImplementedType) {
1345 |           report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1346 |         }
1347 |       }
1348 |       if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1349 |         validator.expectAllInterfacePropertiesImplemented(functionType);
1350 |       }
1351 |     }
1352 |   }

/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
768 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
769 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName) {
770 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
771 |       // on a superclass.
772 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
773 |       if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
774 |         return (FunctionType) propType;

776 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
777 |         // on an implemented interface.

780 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
781 |           if (propType instanceof FunctionType) {
782 |             return (FunctionType) propType;
783 |           }
784 |         }
785 |       }
786 | 
787 |       return null;
788 |     }

===== M0787 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

989 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
990 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
991 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
992 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
993 |       if (relationship != null) {
994 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
995 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
996 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
997 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
998 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
999 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1000 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1001 | 
1002 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
1003 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1004 |           }
1005 | 
1006 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1007 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1008 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1009 |           }
1010 |         }
1011 |       }
1012 | 
1013 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1014 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1015 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1016 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1017 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1018 |         if (objectType != null) {
1019 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1020 | 
1021 |           if (functionType != null) {
1022 |             FunctionType getterType =
1023 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1024 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1025 |                 objectType);
1026 |           }
1027 |         }
1028 |       }
1029 | 
1030 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1031 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1032 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1033 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1034 |       }
1035 | 
1036 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1037 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1038 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1039 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1040 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1041 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1042 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1043 |         } else {
1044 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1045 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1046 |         }
1047 |       }
1048 |     }

/**

===== M0810 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getFunctionType(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 686-763
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the function type from the function node and its attached
     * {@link JSDocInfo}.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param rValue the function node. It must be a {@link Token#FUNCTION}.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
686 |     private FunctionType getFunctionType(String name,
687 |         Node rValue, JSDocInfo info, @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
688 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
689 | 
690 |       // Global function aliases should be registered with the type registry.
691 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
692 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
693 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
694 |           functionType = (FunctionType) var.getType();
695 |           if (functionType != null && functionType.isConstructor()) {
696 |             typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
697 |           }
698 |         }
699 |         return functionType;
700 |       }
701 | 
702 |       Node owner = null;
703 |       if (lvalueNode != null) {
704 |         owner = getPrototypePropertyOwner(lvalueNode);
705 |       }
706 | 
707 |       Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
708 |       boolean isFnLiteral =
709 |           rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
710 |       Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
711 |       Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
712 |           rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
713 |       Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
714 | 
715 |       if (functionType == null && info != null && info.hasType()) {
716 |         JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
717 | 
718 |         // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
719 |         type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
720 |         if (type.isFunctionType()) {
721 |           functionType = (FunctionType) type;
722 |           functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
723 |         }
724 |       }
725 | 
726 |       if (functionType == null) {
727 |         // Find the type of any overridden function.
728 |         FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
729 |         if (lvalueNode != null && lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
730 |             lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
731 |           Var var = scope.getVar(
732 |               lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
733 |           if (var != null) {
734 |             ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
735 |             if (ownerType != null) {
736 |               String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
737 |               overriddenPropType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
738 |             }
739 |           }
740 |         }
741 | 
742 |         functionType =
743 |             new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
744 |                 scope)
745 |             .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
746 |             .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
747 |             .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
748 |             .inferReturnType(info)
749 |             .inferInheritance(info)
750 |             .inferThisType(info, owner)
751 |             .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
752 |             .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
753 |             .buildAndRegister();
754 |       }
755 | 
756 |       // assigning the function type to the function node
757 |       if (rValue != null) {
758 |         setDeferredType(rValue, functionType);
759 |       }
760 | 
761 |       // all done
762 |       return functionType;
763 |     }
