===== M0819 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 134-803
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
134 |   boolean parse() {
135 |     int lineno;
136 |     int charno;
137 | 
138 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
139 |     JSTypeExpression type;
140 | 
141 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
142 |     JsDocToken token = next();
143 | 
144 |     ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
145 |     token = blockInfo.token;
146 | 
147 |     // If we have a block level comment, record it.
148 |     if (blockInfo.string.length() > 0) {
149 |       jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
153 |     retry: for (;;) {
154 |       switch (token) {
155 |         case ANNOTATION:
156 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
157 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
158 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
159 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
160 | 
161 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
162 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
163 |             if (annotation == null) {
164 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
165 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
166 |             } else {
167 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
168 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
169 | 
170 |               switch (annotation) {
171 |                 case AUTHOR:
172 |                   ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
173 |                   String author = authorInfo.string;
174 | 
175 |                   if (author.length() == 0) {
176 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
177 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
178 |                   } else {
179 |                     jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
180 |                   }
181 | 
182 |                   token = authorInfo.token;
183 |                   continue retry;
184 | 
185 |                 case CONSTANT:
186 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
187 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
188 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
189 |                   }
190 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
191 |                   continue retry;
192 | 
193 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
194 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
195 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
196 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
197 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
198 |                     } else {
199 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
200 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
201 |                     }
202 |                   }
203 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
204 |                   continue retry;
205 | 
206 |                 case DEPRECATED:
207 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
208 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
209 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
210 |                   }
211 | 
212 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
213 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
214 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
215 | 
216 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
217 | 
218 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
219 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
220 |                   }
221 | 
222 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
223 |                   continue retry;
224 | 
225 |                 case INTERFACE:
226 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
227 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
228 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
229 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
230 |                     } else {
231 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
232 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
233 |                     }
234 |                   }
235 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
236 |                   continue retry;
237 | 
238 |                 case DESC:
239 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
240 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
241 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
242 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
243 |                     continue retry;
244 |                   } else {
245 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
246 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
247 | 
248 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
249 | 
250 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
251 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
252 |                     continue retry;
253 |                   }
254 | 
255 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
256 |                   ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
257 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
258 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
259 | 
260 |                   String fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
261 | 
262 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
263 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
264 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
265 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
266 |                   }
267 |                   token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
268 |                   continue retry;
269 | 
270 |                 case LICENSE:
271 |                 case PRESERVE:
272 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
273 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
274 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
275 | 
276 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
277 | 
278 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
279 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
280 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
281 |                     }
282 |                   }
283 | 
284 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
285 |                   continue retry;
286 | 
287 |                 case ENUM:
288 |                   token = next();
289 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
290 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
291 | 
292 |                   type = null;
293 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
294 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
295 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
296 |                   }
297 | 
298 |                   if (type == null) {
299 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
300 |                   }
301 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
302 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
303 |                   }
304 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
305 |                   continue retry;
306 | 
307 |                 case EXPORT:
308 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
309 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
310 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
311 |                   }
312 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
313 |                   continue retry;
314 | 
315 |                 case EXTERNS:
316 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
317 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
318 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
319 |                   }
320 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
321 |                   continue retry;
322 | 
323 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
324 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
325 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
326 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
327 |                   }
328 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
329 |                   continue retry;
330 | 
331 |                 case EXTENDS:
332 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
333 |                   skipEOLs();
334 |                   token = next();
335 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
336 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
337 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
338 | 
339 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
340 |                     token = next();
341 |                     matchingRc = true;
342 |                   }
343 | 
344 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
345 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
346 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
347 | 
348 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
349 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
350 | 
351 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
352 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
353 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
354 |                     }
355 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
356 | 
357 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
358 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {
359 |                         parser.addWarning(
360 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
361 |                       }
362 |                     } else {
363 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
364 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
365 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
366 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
367 |                             lineno, charno);
368 |                       }
369 |                     }
370 |                     token = next();
371 |                     if (matchingRc) {
372 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
373 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
374 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
375 |                       }
376 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
377 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
378 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
379 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
380 |                     }
381 |                   } else {
382 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
383 |                   }
384 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
385 |                   continue retry;
386 | 
387 |                 case HIDDEN:
388 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
389 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
390 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
391 |                   }
392 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
393 |                   continue retry;
394 | 
395 |                 case MEANING:
396 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
397 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
398 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
399 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
400 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
401 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
402 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
403 |                   }
404 |                   continue retry;
405 | 
406 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
407 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
408 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
409 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
410 |                   }
411 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
412 |                   continue retry;
413 | 
414 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
415 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
416 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
417 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
418 |                   }
419 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
420 |                   continue retry;
421 | 
422 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
423 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
424 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
425 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
426 |                   }
427 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
428 |                   continue retry;
429 | 
430 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
431 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
432 |                   continue retry;
433 | 
434 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
435 |                 case OVERRIDE:
436 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
437 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
438 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
439 |                   }
440 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
441 |                   continue retry;
442 | 
443 |                 case THROWS:
444 |                   skipEOLs();
445 |                   token = next();
446 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
447 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
448 |                   type = null;
449 | 
450 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
451 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
452 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
453 | 
454 |                     if (type == null) {
455 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
456 |                       // recovering parsing
457 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
458 |                       continue retry;
459 |                     }
460 |                   }
461 | 
462 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
463 |                   token = current();
464 | 
465 |                   // Save the throw type.
466 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
467 | 
468 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
469 |                   ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
470 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
471 | 
472 |                   String description = descriptionInfo.string;
473 | 
474 |                   if (description.length() > 0) {
475 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
476 |                   }
477 | 
478 |                   token = descriptionInfo.token;
479 |                   continue retry;
480 | 
481 |                 case PARAM:
482 |                   skipEOLs();
483 |                   token = next();
484 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
485 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
486 |                   type = null;
487 | 
488 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
489 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
490 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
491 | 
492 |                     if (type == null) {
493 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
494 |                       // recovering parsing
495 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
496 |                       continue retry;
497 |                     }
498 |                     skipEOLs();
499 |                     token = next();
500 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
501 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
502 |                   }
503 | 
504 |                   String name = null;
505 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
506 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
507 |                     token = next();
508 |                   }
509 | 
510 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
511 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
512 |                         lineno, charno);
513 |                   } else {
514 |                     name = stream.getString();
515 | 
516 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
517 |                       token = next();
518 | 
519 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
520 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
521 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
522 |                         token = next();
523 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
524 |                           token = next();
525 |                         }
526 |                       }
527 | 
528 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
529 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
530 |                       } else if (type != null) {
531 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
532 |                         // already.
533 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
534 |                       }
535 |                     }
536 | 
537 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
538 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
539 |                     // for handling properties of params.
540 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
541 |                       name = null;
542 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
543 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
544 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
545 |                             lineno, charno);
546 |                       } else {
547 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
548 |                             lineno, charno);
549 |                       }
550 |                     }
551 |                   }
552 | 
553 |                   if (name == null) {
554 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
555 |                     continue retry;
556 |                   }
557 | 
558 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
559 | 
560 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
561 |                   ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
562 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
563 | 
564 |                   String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
565 | 
566 |                   if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
567 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
568 |                                                             paramDescription);
569 |                   }
570 | 
571 |                   token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
572 |                   continue retry;
573 | 
574 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
575 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
576 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
577 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
578 |                   }
579 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
580 |                   continue retry;
581 | 
582 |                 case PRIVATE:
583 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
584 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
585 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
586 |                   }
587 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
588 |                   continue retry;
589 | 
590 |                 case PROTECTED:
591 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
592 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
593 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
594 |                   }
595 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
596 |                   continue retry;
597 | 
598 |                 case PUBLIC:
599 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
600 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
601 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
602 |                   }
603 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
604 |                   continue retry;
605 | 
606 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
607 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
608 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
609 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
610 |                   }
611 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
612 |                   continue retry;
613 | 
614 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
615 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
616 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
617 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
618 |                   }
619 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
620 |                   continue retry;
621 | 
622 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
623 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
624 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
625 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
626 |                   }
627 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
628 |                   continue retry;
629 | 
630 |                 case SEE:
631 |                   ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
632 |                   String reference = referenceInfo.string;
633 | 
634 |                   if (reference.length() == 0) {
635 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
636 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
637 |                   } else {
638 |                     jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
639 |                   }
640 | 
641 |                   token = referenceInfo.token;
642 |                   continue retry;
643 | 
644 |                 case SUPPRESS:
645 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
646 |                   continue retry;
647 | 
648 |                 case TEMPLATE:
649 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
650 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
651 | 
652 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
653 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
654 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
655 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
656 |                       templateTypeName)) {
657 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
658 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
659 |                   }
660 | 
661 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
662 |                   continue retry;
663 | 
664 |                 case VERSION:
665 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
666 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
667 | 
668 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
669 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
670 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
671 |                   } else {
672 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
673 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
674 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
675 |                     }
676 |                   }
677 | 
678 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
679 |                   continue retry;
680 | 
681 |                 case DEFINE:
682 |                 case RETURN:
683 |                 case THIS:
684 |                 case TYPE:
685 |                 case TYPEDEF:
686 |                   skipEOLs();
687 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
688 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
689 | 
690 |                   token = next();
691 | 
692 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
693 | 
694 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
695 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
696 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
697 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
698 |                     }
699 |                   }
700 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
701 | 
702 |                   if (type == null) {
703 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
704 |                     // recovering parsing
705 |                   } else {
706 |                     switch (annotation) {
707 |                       case DEFINE:
708 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
709 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
710 |                               lineno, charno);
711 |                         }
712 |                         break;
713 | 
714 |                       case RETURN:
715 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
716 |                           parser.addWarning(
717 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
718 |                           break;
719 |                         }
720 | 
721 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
722 |                         token = current();
723 | 
724 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
725 |                         ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
726 |                             extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
727 | 
728 |                         String returnDescription = returnDescriptionInfo.string;
729 | 
730 |                         if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
731 |                           jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
732 |                               returnDescription);
733 |                         }
734 | 
735 |                         token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
736 |                         continue retry;
737 | 
738 |                       case THIS:
739 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
740 |                           parser.addWarning(
741 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
742 |                         }
743 |                         break;
744 | 
745 |                       case TYPE:
746 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
747 |                           parser.addWarning(
748 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
749 |                         }
750 |                         break;
751 | 
752 |                       case TYPEDEF:
753 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
754 |                           parser.addWarning(
755 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
756 |                         }
757 |                         break;
758 |                     }
759 | 
760 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
761 |                   }
762 |                   continue retry;
763 |               }
764 |             }
765 |           }
766 |           break;
767 | 
768 |         case EOC:
769 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
770 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
771 |           }
772 | 
773 |           return true;
774 | 
775 |         case EOF:
776 |           // discard any accumulated information
777 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
778 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
779 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
780 |           return false;
781 | 
782 |         case EOL:
783 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
784 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
785 |           }
786 |           token = next();
787 |           continue retry;
788 | 
789 |         default:
790 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
791 |             token = next();
792 |             continue retry;
793 |           } else {
794 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
795 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
796 |             continue retry;
797 |           }
798 |       }
799 | 
800 |       // next token
801 |       token = next();
802 |     }
803 |   }

===== M0822 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 523-573
Documentation:

Method code:
523 |    @Override
524 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
525 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
526 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
527 |       if (name == null) {
528 |         name = new Name();
529 |         name.setIdentifier("");
530 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
531 |       }
532 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
533 |       Node newName = transform(name);
534 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
535 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
536 |         // declaration.
537 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
538 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
539 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
540 |         // same line as the function keyword.
541 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
542 |             functionNode.getLp();
543 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
544 |       }
545 | 
546 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
547 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
548 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
549 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
550 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
551 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
552 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
553 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
554 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
555 |       if (fnName != null) {
556 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
557 |       } else {
558 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
559 |       }
560 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
561 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
562 | 
563 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
564 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
565 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
566 |       }
567 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
568 | 
569 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
570 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
571 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
572 |      return node;
573 |     }

458 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
459 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();

461 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);

===== M0864 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1452-1491
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1452 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1453 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1454 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1455 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1456 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1457 |       // {?} - right curly
1458 |       // {?=} - equals
1459 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1460 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1461 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1462 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1463 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1464 |       token = next();
1465 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1466 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1467 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1468 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1469 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1470 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1471 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1472 |       }
1473 | 
1474 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1475 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1476 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1477 |     } else {
1478 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1479 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1480 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1481 |           next();
1482 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1483 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1484 |           next();
1485 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1486 |         }
1487 |       }
1488 | 
1489 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1490 |     }
1491 |   }

===== M0867 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 479-525
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
479 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
480 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
481 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
482 |     switch (left.getType()) {
483 |       case Token.NAME:
484 |         String varName = left.getString();
485 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
486 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
487 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
488 |         }
489 | 
490 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
491 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
492 |         // This has two purposes:
493 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
494 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
495 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
496 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
497 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
498 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
499 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
500 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
501 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
502 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
503 |         }
504 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
505 |             resultType : null);
506 | 
507 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
508 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
509 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
510 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 |       case Token.GETPROP:
514 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
515 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
516 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
517 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
518 |               resultType);
519 |         }
520 | 
521 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
522 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
523 |         break;
524 |     }
525 |   }

===== M0873 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 817-922
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.getType() == Token.ASSIGN</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
817 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
818 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
819 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
820 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
821 | 
822 |     if (lvalue.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
823 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
824 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
825 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
826 | 
827 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
828 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
829 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
830 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
831 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
832 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
833 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
834 |         }
835 |       }
836 | 
837 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
838 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
839 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
840 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
841 |             property, rvalue);
842 |         return;
843 |       }
844 | 
845 |       // /** @enum ... */object.name = ...;
846 |       if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
847 |         checkEnumInitializer(
848 |             t, rvalue, info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(
849 |                 t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
850 |         return;
851 |       }
852 | 
853 |       // object.prototype = ...;
854 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
855 |         if (objectJsType instanceof FunctionType) {
856 |           FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) objectJsType;
857 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
858 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
859 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
860 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
861 |           }
862 |         } else {
863 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
864 |         }
865 |         return;
866 |       }
867 | 
868 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
869 |       if (object.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
870 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
871 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
872 | 
873 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
874 |           JSType jsType = object2.getJSType();
875 |           if (jsType instanceof FunctionType) {
876 |             FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) jsType;
877 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
878 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
879 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
880 |             }
881 |           } else {
882 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
883 |           }
884 |           return;
885 |         }
886 |       }
887 | 
888 |       // object.property = ...;
889 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
890 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
891 |       if (type != null) {
892 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
893 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
894 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
895 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
896 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
897 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
898 |         }
899 |         return;
900 |       }
901 |     } else if (lvalue.getType() == Token.NAME) {
902 |       // variable with inferred type case
903 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
904 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
905 |       if (var != null) {
906 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
907 |           return;
908 |         }
909 |       }
910 |     }
911 | 
912 |     // fall through case
913 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
914 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
915 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
916 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
917 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
918 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
919 |     } else {
920 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
921 |     }
922 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1097 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1098 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1099 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1100 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1101 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1102 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1103 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1104 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1105 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1106 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1107 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1108 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1109 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1110 |       return false;
1111 |     }
1112 | 
1113 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1114 |     if (type == null) {
1115 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1116 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1117 |       if (var != null) {
1118 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1119 |         if (varType != null) {
1120 |           type = varType;
1121 |         }
1122 |       }
1123 |     }
1124 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1125 |     return true;
1126 |   }

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:

126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:

129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:

187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

===== M0911 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0913 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 75-641
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
76 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
77 |       return;
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     int type = n.getType();
81 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
82 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
83 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
84 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
85 | 
86 |     // Handle all binary operators
87 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
88 |       Preconditions.checkState(
89 |           childCount == 2,
90 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
91 |           opstr, childCount);
92 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
93 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
94 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
95 | 
96 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
97 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
98 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
99 | 
100 |       // Handle associativity.
101 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
102 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
103 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
104 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
105 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
106 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
107 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
108 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
109 |       } else {
110 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
111 |       }
112 |       return;
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
116 | 
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       case Token.TRY: {
119 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
120 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
121 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
122 | 
123 |         add("try");
124 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
125 | 
126 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
127 |         // isn't a catch block
128 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
129 |         if (catchblock != null) {
130 |           add(catchblock);
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         if (childCount == 3) {
134 |           add("finally");
135 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 |         }
137 |         break;
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
142 |         add("catch(");
143 |         add(first);
144 |         add(")");
145 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.THROW:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
150 |         add("throw");
151 |         add(first);
152 | 
153 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
154 |         // parse this.
155 |         cc.endStatement(true);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.RETURN:
159 |         add("return");
160 |         if (childCount == 1) {
161 |           add(first);
162 |         } else {
163 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
164 |         }
165 |         cc.endStatement();
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.VAR:
169 |         if (first != null) {
170 |           add("var ");
171 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
172 |         }
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
176 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
177 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.NAME:
181 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
182 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
183 |         } else {
184 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
185 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
186 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
187 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
188 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
189 |           } else {
190 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
191 |             // precedence.
192 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 | 
197 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
198 |         add("[");
199 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
200 |         add("]");
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.LP:
204 |         add("(");
205 |         addList(first);
206 |         add(")");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.COMMA:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
211 |         addList(first, false, context);
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NUMBER:
215 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
216 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
220 |       case Token.VOID:
221 |       case Token.NOT:
222 |       case Token.BITNOT:
223 |       case Token.POS:
224 |       case Token.NEG: {
225 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
227 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
228 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
229 |         break;
230 |       }
231 | 
232 |       case Token.HOOK: {
233 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
234 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
235 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
236 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
237 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
239 |         addExpr(last, 1);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.REGEXP:
244 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
245 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
246 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
247 |         }
248 | 
249 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
250 | 
251 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
252 |         if (childCount == 2) {
253 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
254 |         } else {
255 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
256 |           add(regexp);
257 |         }
258 |         break;
259 | 
260 |       case Token.GET_REF:
261 |         add(first);
262 |         break;
263 | 
264 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
265 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
266 |         add(first);
267 |         add(".");
268 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
269 |         break;
270 | 
271 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
272 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
273 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
274 |         }
275 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
276 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
277 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
278 |           add("(");
279 |         }
280 | 
281 |         add("function");
282 |         add(first);
283 | 
284 |         add(first.getNext());
285 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
286 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
287 | 
288 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
289 |           add(")");
290 |         }
291 |         break;
292 | 
293 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
294 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
295 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
296 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
297 |         }
298 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
299 |         if (preserveBlock) {
300 |           cc.beginBlock();
301 |         }
302 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
303 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
304 | 
305 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
306 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
307 |             cc.endStatement();
308 |           }
309 | 
310 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
311 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
312 |           }
313 | 
314 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
315 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
316 |           if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
317 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
318 |           }
319 |         }
320 |         if (preserveBlock) {
321 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
322 |         }
323 |         break;
324 |       }
325 | 
326 |       case Token.FOR:
327 |         if (childCount == 4) {
328 |           add("for(");
329 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
330 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
331 |           } else {
332 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
333 |           }
334 |           add(";");
335 |           add(first.getNext());
336 |           add(";");
337 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
338 |           add(")");
339 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
340 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
341 |         } else {
342 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
343 |           add("for(");
344 |           add(first);
345 |           add("in");
346 |           add(first.getNext());
347 |           add(")");
348 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
349 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
350 |         }
351 |         break;
352 | 
353 |       case Token.DO:
354 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
355 |         add("do");
356 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
357 |         add("while(");
358 |         add(last);
359 |         add(")");
360 |         cc.endStatement();
361 |         break;
362 | 
363 |       case Token.WHILE:
364 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
365 |         add("while(");
366 |         add(first);
367 |         add(")");
368 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
369 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
370 |         break;
371 | 
372 |       case Token.EMPTY:
373 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
374 |         break;
375 | 
376 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
377 |         Preconditions.checkState(
378 |             childCount == 2,
379 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
380 |         Preconditions.checkState(
381 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
382 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
383 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
384 |         if (needsParens) {
385 |           add("(");
386 |         }
387 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
388 |         if (needsParens) {
389 |           add(")");
390 |         }
391 |         add(".");
392 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
393 |         break;
394 |       }
395 | 
396 |       case Token.GETELEM:
397 |         Preconditions.checkState(
398 |             childCount == 2,
399 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
400 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
401 |         add("[");
402 |         add(first.getNext());
403 |         add("]");
404 |         break;
405 | 
406 |       case Token.WITH:
407 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
408 |         add("with(");
409 |         add(first);
410 |         add(")");
411 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
412 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
413 |         break;
414 | 
415 |       case Token.INC:
416 |       case Token.DEC: {
417 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
418 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
419 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
420 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
421 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
422 |         if (postProp != 0) {
423 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
424 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
425 |         } else {
426 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
427 |           add(first);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       case Token.CALL:
433 |         // We have two special cases here:
434 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
435 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
436 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
437 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
438 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
439 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
440 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
441 |         // that must be preserved.
442 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
443 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
444 |           add("(0,");
445 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
446 |           add(")");
447 |         } else {
448 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
449 |         }
450 |         add("(");
451 |         addList(first.getNext());
452 |         add(")");
453 |         break;
454 | 
455 |       case Token.IF:
456 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
457 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
458 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
459 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
460 |           cc.beginBlock();
461 |         }
462 | 
463 |         add("if(");
464 |         add(first);
465 |         add(")");
466 | 
467 |         if (hasElse) {
468 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
469 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
470 |           add("else");
471 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
472 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
473 |         } else {
474 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
475 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         }
477 | 
478 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
479 |           cc.endBlock();
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.NULL:
484 |       case Token.THIS:
485 |       case Token.FALSE:
486 |       case Token.TRUE:
487 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
488 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
489 |         break;
490 | 
491 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
492 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
493 |         add("continue");
494 |         if (childCount == 1) {
495 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
496 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
497 |           }
498 |           add(" ");
499 |           add(first);
500 |         }
501 |         cc.endStatement();
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
505 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
506 |         add("debugger");
507 |         cc.endStatement();
508 |         break;
509 | 
510 |       case Token.BREAK:
511 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
512 |         add("break");
513 |         if (childCount == 1) {
514 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
515 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
516 |           }
517 |           add(" ");
518 |           add(first);
519 |         }
520 |         cc.endStatement();
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
524 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
525 | 
526 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
527 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
528 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
529 |         cc.endStatement();
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.NEW:
533 |         add("new ");
534 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
535 | 
536 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
537 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
538 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
539 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
540 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
541 |         }
542 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
543 | 
544 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
545 |         Node next = first.getNext();
546 |         if (next != null) {
547 |           add("(");
548 |           addList(next);
549 |           add(")");
550 |         }
551 |         break;
552 | 
553 |       case Token.STRING:
554 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
555 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
556 |         break;
557 | 
558 |       case Token.DELPROP:
559 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
560 |         add("delete ");
561 |         add(first);
562 |         break;
563 | 
564 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount % 2 == 0);
566 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
567 |         if (needsParens) {
568 |           add("(");
569 |         }
570 |         add("{");
571 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext().getNext()) {
572 |           if (c != first) {
573 |             cc.listSeparator();
574 |           }
575 | 
576 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
577 |           // not JavaScript keywords
578 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
579 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
580 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
581 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
582 |               // unicode escaped.
583 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
584 |             add(c.getString());
585 |           } else {
586 |             addExpr(c, 1);
587 |           }
588 |           add(":");
589 |           addExpr(c.getNext(), 1);
590 |         }
591 |         add("}");
592 |         if (needsParens) {
593 |           add(")");
594 |         }
595 |         break;
596 |       }
597 | 
598 |       case Token.SWITCH:
599 |         add("switch(");
600 |         add(first);
601 |         add(")");
602 |         cc.beginBlock();
603 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
604 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
605 |         break;
606 | 
607 |       case Token.CASE:
608 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
609 |         add("case ");
610 |         add(first);
611 |         addCaseBody(last);
612 |         break;
613 | 
614 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
615 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
616 |         add("default");
617 |         addCaseBody(first);
618 |         break;
619 | 
620 |       case Token.LABEL:
621 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
622 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
623 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
624 |         }
625 |         add(first);
626 |         add(":");
627 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
628 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
632 |       // ignored for our purposes.
633 |       case Token.SETNAME:
634 |         break;
635 | 
636 |       default:
637 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
641 |   }

===== M0922 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1317-1319
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1317 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1318 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1319 |   }

===== M0924 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 808-835
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
808 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
809 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
810 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
811 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
812 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
813 |           return;
814 |         }
815 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
816 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
817 | 
818 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
819 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
820 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
821 |           } else {
822 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
823 |           }
824 |         } else { // Has a catch.
825 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
826 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
827 |             return;
828 |           } else {
829 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
830 |           }
831 |         }
832 |         lastJump = handler;
833 |       }
834 |     }
835 |   }
